O4 (git-queue deprecation) closed as re-scoped, not implemented — an owner comment and a later commit agree git-queue remains the permanent fallback, not a deprecation target.
- LMCP-O6 (`construct-9oi4.15.9`): resolved the four dead config keys the O2 sweep tracked as a follow-up. `roleSelection.primary/secondary/perConversationOverride` and `hosts.<surface>.enabled` are removed from `lib/config/schema.mjs` (each was already superseded by a separate live mechanism that never read the project-config field — `CONSTRUCT_ROLE_PRIMARY`/`CONSTRUCT_ROLE_SECONDARY` via `roles:set`, and `--hosts=`/`CONSTRUCT_SYNC_HOSTS` via ADR-0027 §1 — so wiring the schema field in would have created two competing sources of truth). `deployment.mcpBroker` and `autoEmbed` were genuine oversights rather than abandoned features, so both are now wired with the same env > project-config > default precedence `deployment.mode` already uses: `isBrokered()` (`lib/mcp/broker.mjs`) reads `config.deployment.mcpBroker`, and `autoStartEmbedIfNeeded()` (`lib/embed/cli.mjs`) reads `config.autoEmbed`, both falling back to the prior env-only gate. `tests/config/key-consumer-parity.test.mjs`'s ALLOWLIST is now empty. Tests: `tests/config/key-consumer-parity.test.mjs`, `tests/mcp-broker.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/deployment-mode-single-resolver.functional.test.mjs`.
- Two stale duplicate `config.protection.violation` security-incident beads (same fingerprint as five already-closed duplicates) verified against a clean `eslint.config.mjs` diff and closed with no code change.
- The entire "Close the chat TUI polish gap" epic (`construct-m4gw`, 11 remaining children + parent) closed as superseded — the `lib/chat/tui/*` substrate every remaining bead's root-cause evidence cited was deleted wholesale by an earlier "Retire Construct terminal chat surface" commit; there is nothing left to migrate, prototype, or harden against.
- `construct-f8w6.3`: re-verified live that `op://` credential materialization from a bare-node MCP context now succeeds on this machine (previously failed non-interactively) via 1Password desktop-app CLI integration; documented the bare-node resolution mechanism in `docs/guides/reference/config.md` (only the `op run`-wrapper path was previously documented there).
- Full-suite audit fixes surfaced while integrating this batch: a doctor false-positive on a captured tool-output fixture that quotes the retired-registry-paths policy as prose; a provider-manifest/graph-node id mismatch (`local-llm.manifest.json` renamed to `local.manifest.json` to match its own declared `id`, matching every other manifest's convention); the MCP tool-doc-parity guard updated to parse the renamed `HARDCODED_TOOL_DEFS` array (plus any self-registered `*.tool.mjs` module) after LMCP-B5's tool-registration refactor; a missing test cleanup signal; a new runbook wired into its docs index and a known-partial dead module baselined against its still-open tracking bead so the audit-ratchet gate reflects reality; an ACP functional test's wait window widened for a real (if slow) provider-backed degraded run — root cause of the ~100s duration tracked separately as `construct-vevd`.

### Fixed (2026-07-05)

- `construct-trxz.12` closed: confirmed via primary source (code.claude.com/docs/en/mcp, "Environment variable expansion in `.mcp.json`") that Claude Code's `${VAR}` env-block expansion — the syntax `buildClaudeMcpEntry`'s existing flip already emits — is a real, documented feature of the `.mcp.json` file format, completing the per-host validation the bead required (OpenCode live-tested, VS Code doc-confirmed unsupported and correctly reverted, Claude doc-confirmed supported). `lib/mcp-platform-config.mjs`'s comment now cites this source. Whether Construct's Claude write target (`~/.claude/settings.json`) is actually consulted by the installed CLI is a separate, already-tracked question (`construct-ranh`) that this bead's literal-vs-reference scope does not resolve. Verified: `node --test tests/mcp-secret-ref.test.mjs tests/mcp-stale-managed-reconcile.test.mjs tests/functional/mcp-add-no-plaintext.functional.test.mjs tests/audit/f02-secrets/generated-file-leak-scan.test.mjs tests/audit/f02-secrets/sync-template-leak.test.mjs tests/mcp-manager.test.mjs` — 37 passed, 0 failed.
- `construct-6y6w.7`: the `orchestration_run` MCP dispatch (`lib/mcp/server.mjs`) now defaults `host` from the `initialize` handshake's `clientInfo.name` (`server.getClientVersion()?.name`) instead of always falling through to `cli-direct` — every MCP surface that sends a real handshake (VS Code, OpenCode, Cursor, ...) now has its own identity recorded in the persisted run's `hostRole`, closing the AP8.4/.6/.9 batch's deferred `.7`. An explicit `host` tool arg still overrides the handshake default, and the CLI path (`bin/construct`, which calls `runOrchestration` directly and never runs an MCP handshake) is unaffected and still records `cli-direct`. Tests: `tests/mcp/host-role-clientinfo.test.mjs`.
- `construct-aduf` (LMCP-L7 wave-3 follow-up): `tests/functional/deck-export.functional.test.mjs`'s `OUT_DIR` was a fixed path under the repo (`.tmp/deck-export-test`), violating `tests/functional/README.md`'s per-test `mkdtempSync` isolation rule and risking cross-run/cross-process collisions; now an `fs.mkdtempSync(os.tmpdir())` directory removed in `test.after`. Test logic (real pptxgenjs/pandoc invocation, real PPTX magic-byte check) unchanged. Tests: `node --test tests/functional/deck-export.functional.test.mjs` — 4 passed, 0 failed.
- `construct-cbl6` (follow-up to `construct-6y6w.1`): the stale-managed-MCP-entry reconcile pass now also covers OpenCode — `reconcileStaleManagedEntries` takes an optional `mapId` to translate a host config key back to the catalog id (OpenCode's `config.mcp` is keyed by `getOpenCodeMcpId(id)`, not the raw id), and both stale predicates (`mcpEntryPointsOutsideToolkit`, `memoryEntryIsStale`) now also read OpenCode's `command`/`environment` entry shape, not just Claude's `args`/`env`. Left Claude/VS Code global scope unwired: `GLOBAL_CLAUDE_MCP_IDS`/`globalMcpAllowlist` is a confirmed-intentional narrower policy (ADR-0031, `platforms/capabilities.json`, a dedicated allowlist test), so using the full managed-id set as reconcile's ownership set there would rewrite entries the global policy deliberately excludes, while restricting ownership to the allowlist itself makes reconcile a guaranteed no-op (the desired set and the ownership set would be identical) — neither variant adds value. Windows path handling is a known separate gap (`construct-bcbo.4`), inherited but not addressed here. Tests: extended `tests/mcp-stale-managed-reconcile.test.mjs`, added `tests/functional/opencode-stale-managed-reconcile.functional.test.mjs`.
- `construct-6y6w.3` (epic close, `construct-6y6w`): restored `bin/construct`'s dropped `shadowWinner: 'shell'` argument to `loadConstructEnv` — the CLI only fills keys missing from `process.env` (a conflicting shell export wins there), but the shadow warning defaulted to `shadowWinner: 'file'` and claimed "the config file will be used," which was false on the CLI surface. The correct argument shipped in `2284206c` (`construct-xj96.12`) but a later same-day commit (`5ad34e0a`) touched the same line and dropped it while resolving an unrelated `PROJECT_ROOT` change; `tests/env-config/shadow-warning.test.mjs` only exercises `shadowWarningMessage()` directly and never spawns `bin/construct`, so the regression was invisible. Live-reproduced the false warning before fixing it. `.3` was the only remaining open child of the `AP8` epic; all nine children plus follow-up `construct-cbl6` are now closed, closing the epic. Tests: `tests/functional/env-shadow-warning-cli.functional.test.mjs` (new, spawns the real binary and asserts the printed warning names the shell value, not the config file).
- `construct-6y6w.8`: the project `.env` tier `lib/env-config.mjs` documents was never honored — `bin/construct` bound `rootDir` to the toolkit install checkout (loading the toolkit's own `.env`, not the user's project `.env`), and `lib/mcp/server.mjs` called `prepareConstructEnv` with no `rootDir` at all. Both surfaces now resolve the actual project root via `lib/roots.mjs`'s `resolveProjectRoot()` (walk up from cwd to a `.cx/` or `package.json` marker) and pass that as `rootDir`, so a `.env` in the user's repo loads with the documented project-over-user-config.env precedence on both the CLI and MCP surfaces. Toolkit-dev-checkout invocations (no `.cx/` ancestor above the toolkit's own `package.json`) are unaffected. Tests: `tests/functional/project-dotenv-tier.functional.test.mjs`.
- `construct-7164`: `guard-bash`'s role-fence check no longer fences a bash command just because SOME persona was dispatched recently — it now requires the CURRENT invocation's actor identity (Claude Code's per-subagent `agent_id`, or a self-declared `CONSTRUCT_AGENT_ID`) to match the identity recorded at dispatch time before enforcing that persona's fence; the anonymous shared `last-agent.json` fallback can no longer drive enforcement on its own. Also shrank the freshness window (10min → 5min) as defense-in-depth and corrected the hook's own `CONSTRUCT_ROLES=off` bypass message, which claimed an inline env-var prefix on the gated command works even in hosts (e.g. Claude Code) where the hook runs as a separate process and never sees it. Tests: `tests/hooks/guard-bash-role-fence.test.mjs`.
- `construct-diq1` (follow-up to `construct-7164`): fixed a `cx-` prefix mismatch between the per-agent fence file `agent-tracker.mjs` writes (`last-agent-cx-<role>.json`, prefix kept) and the filename `guard-bash.mjs`'s self-declared `CONSTRUCT_AGENT_ID` path looked up (`last-agent-<role>.json`, prefix already stripped) — the self-declared-identity path could never find the file the tracker actually wrote. `agent-tracker.mjs` now strips the `cx-` prefix before building the per-agent filename, matching `guard-bash.mjs`'s existing convention (and the `roleId` stripping already used elsewhere in the same tracker file for `recordOutcome`). Latent until now — nothing in this repo's first-party code sets `CONSTRUCT_AGENT_ID` yet. Tests: extended `tests/hooks/guard-bash-role-fence.test.mjs` with a case that spawns the real `agent-tracker.mjs` hook and confirms `guard-bash.mjs` reads the file it actually wrote.
- `construct-1yhp` (epic, closed): closed the last remaining item — `classifyHostReadiness` (`lib/host/readiness.mjs`) was dead code (its sole caller was a fixture test) despite its own header claiming to be "surfaced by construct doctor"; the doctor-wiring half of `construct-bcbo.4`/HOST-003 was never actually landed even though that bead was later closed as superseded. `construct doctor` now calls it for a detected VS Code install (settings.json + project `.vscode/mcp.json`) and prints an advisory `VS Code MCP host readiness: <state>` line (`missing_config`/`stale_path`/`jsonc_unpatched`/`wrong_key`/`disabled`/`healthy`) with its next-step hint — never failing the gate. The other four AP2 items (model/credential/web/backend executability checks, mcp-health-check warn-only, readiness scope-label honesty, dispatch-guard zero-task degradation) were already verified done in the 2026-07-04 LMCP batch. Tests: `tests/functional/doctor-vscode-host-readiness.functional.test.mjs` (4 passing).
- Fixed Claude Code MCP server registration writing to a file the CLI never reads (`construct-ranh`). `lib/mcp-manager.mjs` (`construct mcp add`/`remove`) and `scripts/sync-specialists.mjs`'s global and project sync both wrote MCP server definitions into `mcpServers` inside `~/.claude/settings.json` (global) or `<project>/.claude/settings.json` (project) — confirmed against code.claude.com/docs/en/mcp's installation-scopes table, code.claude.com/docs/en/managed-mcp (settings.json carries only policy keys), and a live repro on this machine (Claude Code v2.1.201): entries written to `~/.claude/settings.json` never appeared in `claude mcp list`/`claude mcp get`, while the same entries written to `~/.claude.json` (user/local scope) or `.mcp.json` (project scope) did. Construct-written MCP servers for Claude Code (context7, github, memory, etc.) were silently inert. Migrated the write path: user/global scope now targets `~/.claude.json`'s top-level `mcpServers` (`getClaudeUserConfigPath`/`loadClaudeUserConfig`/`saveClaudeUserConfig` in `lib/mcp-manager.mjs`; the corresponding block in `scripts/sync-specialists.mjs`'s global sync); project scope now targets `<project>/.mcp.json` (`writeProjectMcpJson`, split out of `writeProjectClaudeSettings`, which now only carries hooks/permissions). `lib/features.mjs`'s MCP-platform detection and `lib/status.mjs`'s `mcpServers` status field were updated to read the corrected location; `lib/host-capabilities.mjs`'s session-detection heuristic checks both locations. `bin/construct install --cleanup-legacy-global` and `lib/uninstall/uninstall.mjs` are unchanged — they still clean up `~/.claude/settings.json`'s legacy `mcpServers` for pre-migration installs, which is exactly the state a pre-fix Construct left behind (follow-up filed as `construct-tyyy` for uninstall's memory-MCP/settings-un-merge categories, which will miss servers written to the new location by a post-fix install). Tests updated across `tests/mcp-manager.test.mjs`, `tests/sync-contract.test.mjs`, `tests/status.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/host-config-parity.functional.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/mcp-add-no-plaintext.functional.test.mjs`, and `tests/helpers/sterile-host-env.mjs` (added `~/.claude.json` to the real-config drift guard).

### Decisions

- `construct-u5lv`: removed the orphaned `lib/providers/contract/adapters/{git,slack}` adapters and their tests (`tests/provider-git.test.mjs`, `tests/provider-slack.test.mjs`) as dead code rather than wiring them into the governed-write control plane. `bd show construct-9oi4.10` (LMCP-J, now 6/6 closed) confirms the governed-write epic's scope was always GitHub/Jira/Confluence only; `lib/writes/write-intent.mjs`'s `KNOWN_PROVIDERS` is deliberately frozen to those three, and neither `lib/writes/control-plane.mjs` nor `lib/mcp/tools/provider-write.mjs` ever registered a git or slack adapter factory. The `git` contract adapter has no `write()` method at all (capabilities: read/search only), so it cannot be wired into a write control plane without inventing write semantics nothing requests. The `slack` contract adapter does implement `write()`, but unlike jira/github/confluence it never got a `governed-write.mjs` wrapper, a `manifest.json`, or a `tests/writes/*.functional.test.mjs` — it stalled at the raw-adapter stage with no ADR, CHANGELOG entry, or `deps/intent.json` record asking for it to be finished. Along the way, corrected a false claim in `docs/guides/reference/providers/overview.md` (introduced by the LMCP-O2/O3/O5 sweep) that a Slack governed-write adapter shipped `write`/`watch` under construct-9oi4.10 — it never did; `watch` is not implemented in either provider layer. Verified: `node --test tests/provider-framework.test.mjs tests/embed-snapshot.test.mjs tests/writes/*.test.mjs` all pass with the two adapters gone.
- LMCP Track B/P/F decisions (ADR-0060..0062, 2026-07-03): three Opus-authored decision beads closed with ADRs. ADR-0060 (LMCP-B10, `construct-9oi4.2.10`): provider filter DSL — a normalized portable core (`scope` container + closed predicate set `assignee`/`statusCategory`/`priority`/`label`/`updatedSince`) plus optional provider-native `nativeQuery` passthrough (conjunction, least-privilege), filter values in `.cx` validated against the manifest `configSchema`, provider-side pushdown + mandatory plane-side predicate (a filtered-out item never becomes an observation), per-poll `filterApplied` audit. ADR-0061 (LMCP-P1, `construct-9oi4.16.1`): embed capability as a `type:"embed"` workflow-manifest specialization reusing the D1 loader, pack-default/`.cx`-override config home, and a `runtime` selector (`in-process` zero-dependency default via the existing model path, optional `external` host, honest `skipped-with-reason` when absent). ADR-0062 (LMCP-F6, `construct-9oi4.6.5`): persona reasoning framework format — an ordered list of named reasoning moves (`{id, move, question, emits, cites}`) resolved through pack E1 precedence, whose ordered `emits` fingerprint makes specialist framing framework-attributable and testable. ADRs indexed in `docs/decisions/adr/README.md` and `meta.json`.

### Added

- LMCP-B6: orchestration run-store selection now resolves through the extension manifest registry (`kind:"storage"`) instead of accepting only hardcoded names. Builtin `filesystem`/`sqlite`/`postgres` behavior is unchanged, project/user manifests can alias to a supported run-store implementation for fixtures or future providers, and explicit missing storage backends degrade visibly with `requestedBackend` and `degradedReason` rather than silently falling through to a default. Tests: `tests/orchestration-store-resolver.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-G1: `DATABASE_URL`/`CONSTRUCT_DATABASE_URL` now create a real optional Postgres.js SQL client instead of a permanent null stub, with pool/timeout/SSL env tuning, `construct db status|migrate`, and an idempotent migration scaffold for shared orchestration run storage. Solo/no-URL mode still never opens a SQL client and continues to degrade cleanly. Tests: `tests/storage-backend.test.mjs`, `tests/db-migrations.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/sql-client.functional.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-G2/G4: Postgres is now a registered optional `kind:"queue"` provider with durable queue tables, `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` claims, lease expiry/reclaim, heartbeat renewal, and terminal processed/skipped states. `CONSTRUCT_INTAKE_QUEUE_BACKEND=postgres` fails loud without a configured SQL client rather than falling back to git. The Postgres run store is wired for cross-instance round-trips through the migration-owned schema. Tests: `tests/pg-queue.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/pg-queue.functional.test.mjs`, `tests/orchestration-run-store-postgres.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-G3/G5: team and enterprise modes now default the intake queue to Postgres instead of git, fail closed when no SQL client is configured, and require `CONSTRUCT_DEGRADED_OK=postgres-queue` for a visible git fallback. Added a migration-owned worker registry plus `WorkerRegistry` and `construct workers list [--json]` so workers can register identity, refresh heartbeat TTLs, deregister, and report stale liveness. Tests: `tests/intake-queue-factory.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/mode-honesty.functional.test.mjs`, `tests/worker-runtime.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-G6/G9: Postgres queue reliability now has explicit `fail()` retry/backoff handling, max-attempt dead-lettering, cancellation requests surfaced at heartbeat boundaries, idempotent completion keys, and `queueStats()` for operator health. `construct status` and `construct doctor` read shared team queue/worker health, with team-mode missing Postgres as a hard doctor failure and DLQ/stale workers as visible warnings. Added `dev/team-harness/verify.sh` for local team-mode verification. Tests: `tests/pg-queue-reliability.test.mjs`, `tests/team-health.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-N8: adversarial/security corpus wired into the living graph (`construct-9oi4.14.8`) — security tests are now first-class graph nodes instead of a disconnected side list. A `@owasp LLM01` tag (OWASP GenAI Top-10 category ids, comma-separable) makes a test carry its categories; `@secures <workflow-or-preset-id>` adds a `test --secures--> workflow|embed` edge (the seeder resolves embed presets to their `embed:` node). `lib/graph/security-coverage.mjs` generates the coverage matrix and gap list from the graph: `construct graph owasp` lists all ten categories with test counts (uncovered categories show `0`, never silently absent), `construct graph missing-tests --security` lists workflows/presets with no `secures` edge. `graph validate` flags a `@secures` naming a nonexistent node (strict error → covered by the C8 gate). Tagged the existing corpus truthfully (ssrf→LLM06, audit-redaction→LLM02, vector-poisoning→LLM04/LLM08 + research-synthesis, trust-labels/f08→LLM01, destructive-gate/enforcement-proof→LLM06); the three embed presets + research-synthesis + evidence-ingest are covered. Uncovered categories (LLM03/05/07/09/10) and the 9 uncovered executable workflows are tracked as an explicit gap bead (`construct-9oi4.14.9`) rather than assumed safe. New `secures` edge rel + `graph query --type` reuse. Tests: `tests/security/owasp-coverage.test.mjs` (4).
- LMCP-N7: SSRF / DNS-rebinding egress guard (`construct-9oi4.14.7`) — new `lib/net-guard.mjs` is the single chokepoint for attacker-influenceable outbound HTTP. `resolveGuardedTarget` (pure, DNS injectable) validates the URL scheme and classifies *every* resolved address, denying loopback/private/link-local (incl. the `169.254.169.254` metadata address)/CGNAT/reserved ranges by default with a named `SsrfBlockedError.reason`; `guardedFetch` connects to the address validated at check time (pinned IP, original hostname preserved as TLS servername + `Host`), so a DNS record that rebinds to a private address between check and connect cannot move the socket. Private egress is an explicit audited opt-in (`CONSTRUCT_NET_ALLOW_PRIVATE_EGRESS=1` or per-provider `allowPrivateEgress`). Wired into the Jira and Confluence governed-write transports; complements the I5 inbound Host/Origin allowlist + bearer-audience checks. Tests: `tests/security/ssrf.test.mjs` (9) — blocked-range refusal, rebinding-pins-the-validated-address, IP-literal blocking, mixed-address whole-refusal, a live-socket gate, and a provider transport refusing a private baseUrl. Security doc updated.
- LMCP-P6: embed nodes in the living graph (`construct-9oi4.16.6`) — embedded specialists are no longer invisible to the graph. `lib/graph/build-from-embed.mjs` seeds one `embed:<id>` node per loaded `type:"embed"` manifest with edges into the nodes the registry seeder already produces — `embed --uses--> provider` (per `providerBindings`), `embed --owned_by--> specialist`, `embed --governed_by--> contract` — plus an inbound `test --validates--> embed` edge sourced from a new `@embed <id>` acceptance-test annotation (the embed analog of C10's `@capability`, `extractEmbedTestEdges`). `graph validate` gains embed-integrity rules (LMCP-P6): a binding whose target node is absent, or an embed with zero validating tests, is a strict-mode error — so the C8 gate now protects every preset (a deleted preset test or a broken binding fails CI). New `graph query --type <t>` lists all nodes of a type (`--type embed` enumerates the presets). The three shipped presets (`operations`, `operations-triage`, `pm-feedback`) each carry the `@embed` tag. `embed` added to the store node taxonomy and ADR-0053. Tests: `tests/graph/embed-nodes.test.mjs` (4).
- LMCP-C8: CI graph-drift gate (`construct-9oi4.3.8`) — `scripts/run-graph-gate.mjs` (`npm run graph:gate`) rebuilds the living workflow/capability graph and runs `graph validate` in STRICT mode plus the `graph stale` per-source check, failing on any drift: a workflow with zero tests, a capability with a missing doc, a provider without a manifest, or a declared-but-unregistered surface. Strict mode is forced so CI never inherits solo-mode leniency — a gap that is a warning locally is a merge blocker in CI. Added as an **unconditional** step in the ci.yml `lint` job (no change-filter `if:`, so no code path can merge drift by skipping it) and chained into `release:check`; a `CHANGED_FILES`-driven C4 impact annotation names impacted workflows on the PR. Now the ninth check in the CONTRIBUTING pre-PR gate. Self-test `tests/scripts/graph-gate.test.mjs` (3) covers clean-tree pass, strict-vs-solo drift classification, and the gate-honesty (no-`if:`) invariant.
- LMCP-J6: control-plane-only write execution (`construct-9oi4.10.6`) — external writes are now structurally recommend-vs-execute separated. A specialist, embed capability, or MCP caller may only produce a `writeIntent` (`lib/writes/write-intent.mjs`: schema-and-validation only, zero I/O, importable from any orchestration/embed path without smuggling in write access) and hand it to the durable F5/I2 `ApprovalQueue`. `lib/writes/control-plane.mjs` is the sole module that turns an `approved` queue record into a `writeWithEnvelope()` call through the J2 governed envelope — `drainApprovedWriteIntents()` resolves the matching `governed-write` adapter and executes exactly once per record (the envelope's idempotency key + sent-log dedup make a re-drain a cache hit), never for `awaiting_approval`/`denied` records. An import-guard test proves no path outside `lib/writes/` reaches a governed adapter for a queue-sourced intent, so write escalation via prompt injection lands in the approval queue instead of the provider. Tests: `tests/writes/control-plane-execution.functional.test.mjs` (3) + `tests/writes/import-guard.test.mjs` (3).
- LMCP-I5: stdio/HTTP transport separation + remote MCP authorization (`construct-9oi4.9.5`) — the MCP server now resolves an explicit, non-overlapping transport mode (`lib/mcp/transport/mode.mjs`): `stdio-local` is the default and byte-for-byte the original stdio path (local OS identity, no network authn); `http-remote` is opt-in only via `CONSTRUCT_MCP_TRANSPORT=http` and fails closed at startup (`process.exit(1)`, not a degraded keep-alive) when its auth config is absent — no skip/allow env var, the absence of config *is* the refusal. `lib/mcp/transport/auth.mjs` supplies pure, socket-free guards applied per request by `http.mjs`: Host + Origin allowlists (DNS-rebinding defense, pairs with N7 SSRF work), RFC 8707 bearer-audience binding (a token minted for another audience is rejected), and a scoped-context mint that drops the inbound token so passthrough downstream is structurally impossible. Tests: `tests/mcp/transport-separation.test.mjs` (24).
- LMCP-B12: `construct provider add|configure` (`construct-9oi4.2.12`) — per-provider instance config is now a first-class, git-trackable artifact at `.cx/providers/<id>.json` (`{ providerId, config, updatedAt }`, one file per id), distinct from the registry that maps an id to a provider module. `add <id>` scaffolds config from the `configSchema` defaults; `configure <id> --<key.path> <value> ...` merges + validates against the schema and the ADR-0060 `filter` block (delegated to B11's `validateFilterConfig` so the two validators never drift), rejecting with a JSON-Pointer-style path naming the offending key. Validation is a hand-rolled JSON-Schema draft 2020-12 subset (no engine dependency), mirroring `contract.mjs`. `provider status` gains an active-filter column. `lib/providers/instance-config.mjs`; JSON flags throughout. Tests: `tests/providers/provider-add-configure.test.mjs` (10, real binary spawn against an isolated tmpdir; jira/github/slack manifests).
- LMCP-C3: `construct graph explain <workflow>` answering the 12 ownership questions (`construct-9oi4.3.3`) — renders the workflow node plus one section per `EDGE_RELS` relation (grouped by the Q1–Q12 ownership questions) and a manifest-sourced `roleChain` section; a relation with zero applicable edges prints `MISSING` so the reader sees an explicit gap, and every MISSING/violation marker has a matching `graph validate` finding (they share the same gap-query aggregation). `--json` output. `lib/graph/cli.mjs`. Tests: `tests/graph/explain.test.mjs` (6).
- LMCP-P5: `pm-feedback` embed preset (`construct-9oi4.16.5`) — the product-manager specialist reads customer feedback (B14 provider) + PRDs (Confluence), clusters feedback by theme, scores each with the F7 user-value rubric, cross-references PRD requirements, and emits a provenance-tracked `pm-requirements-candidates` artifact. Artifact-only (no writes); untrusted feedback text is carried strictly as quoted evidence, never instruction (a prompt-injection fixture proves it). Tests: `tests/acceptance/pm-feedback-preset.acceptance.test.mjs` (4). Third shipped builtin embed preset.
- LMCP-B8: `construct provider health|status|validate` (`construct-9oi4.2.8`) — `health [id]` runs probes and exits non-zero on failure; `status` is `list` + circuit-breaker/degradation columns; `validate <path|id>` runs the B1 manifest validator. JSON flags throughout; fixed a latent breaker-key-prefix bug. Tests: `tests/providers/provider-commands.test.mjs` (9).
- LMCP-D4: `construct graph validate` now checks workflow surface parity (`construct-9oi4.4.4`) — each workflow manifest's declared `surfaces` must match its actual CLI/MCP registration (both dispatch through the same `invokeWorkflow` core); an undeclared mismatch is an error, and a `surfaceExceptions.<surface>` with a reason string passes with an info note. `lib/workflows/surface-parity.mjs`. Tests: `tests/workflows/surface-parity.test.mjs` (12).
- LMCP-C9: runtime-evidence edges connect the living graph to real execution history (`construct-9oi4.3.9`) — `construct graph build` ingests persisted orchestration runs and emits `runtime-evidence --evidenced_by--> workflow` edges carrying timestamp/outcome; `construct graph explain <workflow>` shows static requirements + last-execution data; a new execution-staleness dimension (`lib/graph/staleness.mjs` `checkExecutionStaleness`) flags never-run workflows distinctly from evidence aged past a per-workflow threshold (default 30d). Tests: `tests/graph/evidence.test.mjs` (17).
- LMCP-F4: orchestration runs aggregate every task's `executionState` (F1) into a run-level `executionState` (`prepared|executed|degraded-executed|failed`) and `construct status` surfaces a "Recent runs" breakdown distinguishing prepare-only from executed runs (`construct-9oi4.6.4`). Tests: `tests/orchestration/execution-state.test.mjs` (7) + status tests.
- LMCP-B14: feedback / customer-input data-source provider (`construct-9oi4.2.14`) — reads a JSONL or CSV drop-directory with caller-configured field mapping, normalizing rows into `feedback-item` records with file+row provenance and an N1 `EXTERNAL_UNAUTHENTICATED` trust label; malformed rows skip with a per-file warning count, never a crash; registered purely via `lib/extensions/manifests/feedback.manifest.json` (zero central-dispatch edits). Tests: `tests/providers/feedback-provider.test.mjs` (22).
- LMCP-P3 (flagship): TPM embed preset (`construct-9oi4.16.3`) — a core-pack embed manifest `operations` (type:embed, specialist cx-operations; read bindings jira+confluence+slack, proposal `atlassian-jira.createIssue`, cadence PT4H, runtime auto) at `lib/embedded-contract/workflows/operations.manifest.json`. New `lib/embed/presets/tpm.mjs` is a deterministic reasoningExecutor (F5-injectable) that composes the F7 ops framework over the bound snapshot: PRD→requirement parse, epic→requirement coverage matrix, uncovered→missing-work finding + approval-gated draft `createIssue` writeIntent, due-date/dependency-chain timeline-risk findings with evidence links, and Slack contradiction/blocker cross-reference — every load-bearing briefing claim carries a re-verifiable source id (no-fabrication). Output validated against the new `operations-tpm-briefing` contract. Embed manifests are now excluded from the executable-workflow catalog/liveness/drift guard (they carry an embed block, not a role chain). Tests: `tests/acceptance/tpm-preset.acceptance.test.mjs` (4, on J1 fakes + new `tests/fakes/fake-slack.mjs`) — seeded PRD/Jira mismatch → queued draft with zero adapter writes; seeded slipping chain → evidence-cited timeline risk; enable→approval→fake ticket end-to-end.
- LMCP-P4: ops-triage embed preset (`construct-9oi4.16.4`) — a second core-pack embed preset (`operations-triage`) that walks new/changed Jira tickets for duplicates (type+component+similarity) and under-specification, queuing typed approval-gated proposals (duplicate-link, needs-info comment); zero writes pre-approval. `lib/embed/presets/ops-triage.mjs` + manifest + `operations-triage` output contract. Tests: `tests/acceptance/ops-triage-preset.acceptance.test.mjs` (4).
- LMCP-C4: `construct graph impacted --changed <files>` (`construct-9oi4.3.4`) — traverses imports/realizes/embeds/validates/documents edges from changed files to impacted workflows, tests, docs, and capabilities (`--json`); unknown files warn, not crash. Backs the C8 CI drift gate. Tests: `tests/graph/impacted.test.mjs` (5).
- LMCP-C5: six read-only `construct graph` gap-query subcommands (`construct-9oi4.3.5`), each `--json`: `missing-tests`, `missing-docs`, `stale`, `dependencies`, `providers`, `surfaces`; `missing-tests` is guaranteed consistent with `graph validate`'s error list. Tests: `tests/graph/gap-queries.test.mjs` (8).
- LMCP-I7: MCP `provider_write` tool (`construct-9oi4.9.7`) — governed external write to jira/confluence/github contract adapters, classified destructive. `dry_run` (default true) returns a validation-only diff via each adapter's `renderDryRun`, never touching the write path; executing requires the destructive-gate approval token and dispatches through the J2 envelope (idempotency/dedup/audit) to the adapter, never directly; respects E4 `embedBindings` when called with a `specialist_id`. Tests: `tests/mcp/provider-write-tool.test.mjs` (15).
- LMCP-I4: functional proof tests for MCP policy enforcement (`construct-9oi4.9.4`) — 13 tests with a side-effect-recording fixture proving denied calls never execute (durable denied record) and approval-required calls pause until approved then execute exactly once, and that no broker-bypass path exists. `tests/mcp/enforcement-proof.functional.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-B13: local-directory read-only context provider (`construct-9oi4.2.13`) — `lib/providers/directory/index.mjs` (meta/configSchema/health/read/search over include/exclude globs with maxFileKB) + `lib/extensions/manifests/directory.manifest.json` (data-source, trusted, no secrets), registered via the manifest loader with zero central-dispatch edits; rejects `..` traversal and symlink escape from the configured root. Tests: `tests/providers/directory-provider.test.mjs` (26).
- LMCP-F8: workflow-invoke plan assembly resolves the bound specialist's ADR-0062 reasoning framework via pack precedence (`construct-9oi4.6.7`), injecting ordered step prompts and `emits`-derived required output fields into the plan; traces record `frameworkId`/`version`/`source`, and a role with no authored framework surfaces a visible `framework-missing` degradation flag instead of a silent fallback. Differentiation tests assert disjoint, framework-attributable `emits` fingerprints (product-manager vs operations). `lib/embedded-contract/workflow-invoke.mjs`; tests in `tests/functional/perspective-differentiation.functional.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-F3: perspective-differentiation regression guard (`construct-9oi4.6.3`) — a deterministic functional test proving two specialists receive materially different system prompts for the same request, plus a negative control confirming persona-fallback convergence (E2 `personaAvailable`/`degraded`) is detected, not silently accepted.
- LMCP-F2: worker-boundary contract enforcement (`construct-9oi4.6.2`) — `runTaskViaProvider` validates opt-in handoff packets (`task.packet`) before invoking a provider (hard-block on `CONTRACT_VIOLATION_INPUT`) and opt-in output packets after (marks `contractStatus: 'contract-failed'` without discarding real model output); reuses the existing `validatePacket`/`specialists/org/contracts` machinery, violations land in `.cx/contract-violations.jsonl`. Tests: `tests/contracts-worker-boundary.test.mjs` (11).
- LMCP-J3: Jira write adapter (`construct-9oi4.10.3`) — issue create + comment through the J2 governed write envelope, with createmeta pre-validation, Atlassian Document Format (ADF) body construction, and actionable project/permission error mapping; envelope-only exposure, dry-run renders the ADF payload. `lib/providers/contract/adapters/jira/*`; tests `tests/writes/jira.functional.test.mjs` (14).
- LMCP-J4: Confluence publish/update through the J2 governed write envelope (`construct-9oi4.10.4`) — title+space duplicate detection returns a linkback instead of a second page, updates recover from 409 version conflicts via refetch+re-render+re-approve, and successful writes leave a linkback footer comment. `lib/providers/contract/adapters/confluence/*`; tests `tests/writes/confluence.functional.test.mjs` (13).
- LMCP-N4: vector-store poisoning defenses on the retrieval path (`construct-9oi4.14.4`). New `lib/storage/retrieval-hardening.mjs` composes sanity-threshold → duplicate-collapse → trust-weighted-ranking (reads N1 `_trust.level`) → per-source recall cap, wired into `lib/storage/hybrid-query.mjs`; `flagRetrievalAnomalies` adds a leave-one-out z-score check for outlier per-source retrieval frequency. Tests: `tests/security/vector-poisoning.test.mjs` (14, extends the F08 injection corpus) proving a poisoned source cannot exceed its recall cap and untrusted results rank below trusted at equal similarity.
- LMCP-F1: full execution provenance in task traces (2026-07-03, `construct-9oi4.6.1`). `lib/orchestration/worker.mjs`/`runtime.mjs` records `specialistId`, `packId`, `promptVersion` (content hash of the resolved persona), `model`, `provider`, `toolGrants`, and `executionState` (`prepared`/`executed`/`degraded-executed`/`failed`) on task records and the `.cx/traces` `worker.completed` event; additive and backward compatible (pre-F1 records read as typed absence). Tests: `tests/orchestration/provenance.test.mjs` (10).
- LMCP-F7: five persona reasoning frameworks (product-manager, operations, engineer, qa, architect) per ADR-0062, resolved through pack E1 precedence (`specialists/org/frameworks/`, project `.cx/packs` override). New `lib/frameworks/schema.mjs` validator + `lib/frameworks/loader.mjs`; framework `emits` fields bound additively into `specialists/org/contracts/*.json`. Tests: `tests/frameworks-schema.test.mjs` (32).
- LMCP-C11: `construct graph validate` now performs workflow-manifest liveness checks (`construct-9oi4.3.11`) — roleChain entries resolve to installed specialists (pack-aware), skill files exist, handoff graphs are acyclic, and every workflow declares a reachable surface+mode; violations are errors in team/enterprise, warnings in solo (`lib/workflows/liveness.mjs`, wired into `lib/graph/validate.mjs`). Tests: `tests/workflows/liveness.test.mjs` (9).
- LMCP-K2: governed ingestion providers (`construct-9oi4.11.2`) — docling and whisper as `ingestion-provider` manifests with probed install state, health check, and a declared degradation chain (docling → node-native → refuse). `lib/ingest/degraded-extract.mjs` marks results `degraded` when the chain falls through; `lib/doctor/sidecar-providers.mjs` reports install+health in present/absent states. uv is an install dependency of docling only. Tests: `tests/ingest/sidecar-providers.test.mjs` (23).
- LMCP-J5: GitHub issue writes route through the J2 governed write envelope (`construct-9oi4.10.5`). `lib/providers/contract/adapters/github/governed-write.mjs` adds cross-run duplicate detection (hidden idempotency marker matched via issue search → linkback instead of a new issue) and secondary-rate-limit (403 + retry-after) handling that retries bounded then surfaces; `index.mjs` now checks `gh` exit status honestly. Tests: `tests/writes/github.functional.test.mjs` (5).
- LMCP-L9: test coverage for the oracle staleness machinery (`construct-9oi4.12.9`) — `tests/oracle-reconcile-staleness.test.mjs`, `tests/oracle-gaps-detection.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/oracle-heartbeat.functional.test.mjs` (43 tests) covering SLA-window boundaries both sides, changed-capability gap detection, and heartbeat lifecycle with an injected clock. No production changes.
- LMCP-B11: provider filters enforced at poll/read time (`construct-9oi4.2.11`, implements ADR-0060). The daemon snapshot cycle (`lib/embed/daemon.mjs` `enforceSectionFilters`) resolves each source's `filter`, pushes down to Jira (JQL via `lib/embed/providers/jira.mjs` `buildJqlFromFilter`) and applies the plane-side predicate universally, dropping non-matching items before distillation and appending a `{provider, instance, filterHash, matched, dropped}` audit line; unknown filter keys fail the source closed. New `lib/providers/filter-schema.mjs` + `filter-audit.mjs`; `validateFilterConfig`/`matchesFilter` in `lib/providers/contract.mjs`. Also fixed a latent bug where `distillSnapshotItems` never awaited `searchObservations`, so no observations were ever written. Tests: `tests/functional/provider-filter-enforcement.functional.test.mjs` (7).
- LMCP-E4: per-specialist provider read + write-proposal authorization (`construct-9oi4.5.4`). Pack manifests gain an `embedBindings` block validated against known extension manifests at load; `AuthorityGuard` denies any embed proposal outside a specialist's grant before it reaches the approval queue; bindings surface as `specialist --reads--> provider` edges in `construct graph`. Closes the excessive-agency gap where any embedded specialist could read any provider or propose any write. Core pack ships default bindings for product-manager, operations, engineer. Tests: `tests/embed-authority-binding.test.mjs` (9).
- LMCP-N3: audit-trail secret redaction (`construct-9oi4.14.3`). `lib/audit-trail.mjs` runs a redact-then-sign pass before chain-hash/append — known secret values (hashed compare against the secret-resolver cache) and token patterns (`ghp_`/`xoxb`/`sk-`/`op://`/bearer) become `<redacted:kind>` across broker audit, write-envelope audit, and sensitive trace payloads; the signature chain stays valid post-redaction and the redaction is itself audited by count. Tests: `tests/security/audit-redaction.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-P2: embed-capability loader + `construct embed enable|disable|status|list|dry-run` lifecycle (2026-07-03, `construct-9oi4.16.2`, implements ADR-0061). New `lib/embed/capability-loader.mjs` discovers `type:"embed"` workflow manifests via the D1 three-tier loader (`lib/workflows/loader.mjs`: builtin, every pack's `workflows/` dir, project `.cx/embed/`) and validates the `embed` block (`specialist`, `providerBindings`, `filter`, `framework`, `outputContract`, `proposalAuthority`, `cadence`, `runtime`) against the ADR-0061 shape, reusing `lib/providers/filter-schema.mjs` and the read-only `validateFilterConfig` from `lib/providers/contract.mjs` for the ADR-0060 filter block — every failure returns a JSON-schema-style field path (e.g. `embed.providerBindings[1]`, `embed.filter.scope.repos`) and nothing invalid is ever written. `lib/workflows/manifest-schema.mjs` `WORKFLOW_TYPES` gains `'embed'` (additive) so the D1 loader accepts the specialization wholesale, per the ADR's explicit reuse decision. New `lib/embed/capability-lifecycle.mjs` implements `enableCapability`/`disableCapability` (persist to `.cx/embed/<id>.manifest.json`; enablement is explicit opt-in — `embed.enabled === true` only, never a default), `listCapabilities`, `capabilityStatus`, and `resolveCapabilityChain` (the dry-run resolver: specialist → providers → filter → framework → authority → runtime, no side effects). New `lib/embed/capability-runtime.mjs` resolves the `runtime` selector (`in-process | external | auto | none`) per ADR-0061 §3 — `auto` resolves to `in-process` via the existing model-resolution path (`lib/model-router.mjs` `readCurrentModels`, zero new dependency), or `external` if `CONSTRUCT_EXTERNAL_AGENT_HOST` is configured, or `none` with a visible `skipped-with-reason(no-runtime)` — never fabricated output. New `lib/embed/capability-jobs.mjs` `registerEmbedCapabilityJobs` reads the enabled set and registers exactly one `Scheduler` job per enabled capability (wired into `EmbedDaemon.start()` in `lib/embed/daemon.mjs` as Job 16, surfaced in `daemon.status().embedCapabilities`); each job body is this bead's stub — it resolves the runtime and records a durable `skipped-with-reason` tick (`.cx/runtime/embed-capabilities/<id>.json`), since the actual reasoning call lands in LMCP-F5. `lib/embed/cli.mjs` gains `list|enable|disable|dry-run` subcommands and extends `status` to accept an optional `<id>` for per-capability status (bindings/filter/runtime/last-tick), leaving bare `embed status` as the existing daemon-status behavior; catalog entry updated in `lib/cli-commands.mjs`. Tests: `tests/embed-capability-loader.test.mjs` (42 tests: validation, D1 tiering/merge, enable/disable round-trip, dry-run purity, runtime resolution, job registration) and `tests/functional/embed-capability-lifecycle.functional.test.mjs` (6 tests spawning the real `bin/construct` binary against an isolated tmpdir, asserting on the durable `.cx/embed/` and daemon-registration artifacts) — 48 total, all passing.
- LMCP-E2: hard-fail missing prompts for team/enterprise packs; visible solo degraded fallback (2026-07-03, `construct-9oi4.5.2`). `lib/orchestration/worker.mjs` `loadPersona()` no longer silently substitutes a generic one-liner persona on a miss — it resolves personas ONLY through the pack registry (`lib/packs/prompts.mjs` `resolvePersonaPrompt`, new module) and, in team/enterprise deployment mode, refuses outright with `PERSONA_UNAVAILABLE` naming the missing prompt file. `lib/packs/loader.mjs` (`loadPacksFromDir`/`loadAllPacks`) validates every declared prompt file + YAML frontmatter (`validatePackPrompts`) at load time; under team/enterprise mode a broken pack is rejected there, before any run — the error names the missing file. Solo mode still degrades: a miss returns `personaAvailable: false` and `degraded: 'persona-fallback'` on the `runTaskViaProvider` result, which `lib/orchestration/runtime.mjs` `executeRun` carries onto the task, into the `worker.completed` trace metadata (unconditionally, independent of `chainOfThought` mode), and into `run.degraded`/`run.degradationReason` (terminal status `degraded`). `lib/status.mjs` `buildStatus` adds `personaDegradedRuns: {total, runs}` (reads `.cx/runtime/orchestration/runs/` directly, best-effort) and `formatStatusReport` prints a `Persona-degraded runs: N` line when non-zero. Pack prompt paths resolve relative to each pack's own directory (`_packDir`), not a single shared root, and the registry is sorted project-before-user-before-builtin (ADR-0055) before a role lookup — a project-tier-override regression test caught the initial pass resolving to the wrong tier. `loadAllPacks`'s core-pack loader also now reads from `packageRoot` rather than the caller's `rootDir`, fixing a latent bug where any caller passing a project root produced an empty core pack (zero specialists, zero prompts). Tests: `tests/packs/prompt-validation.test.mjs` (22 tests), `tests/orchestration/persona-degraded.test.mjs` (13 tests) — 35 total, all passing.
- `construct-9oi4.5` (LMCP-E epic close): all four children (E1 `construct-9oi4.5.1` pack manifest schema/loader/validator, E2 `construct-9oi4.5.2` hard-fail missing prompts, E3 `construct-9oi4.5.3` pack versioning/compat + enable/disable lifecycle, E4 `construct-9oi4.5.4` specialist↔provider capability binding) were already closed individually but the epic itself was left open — no further code gap found against its success criteria (versioned/validated pack units, hard-fail on missing prompts in team/enterprise, core pack wraps the existing org with zero behavior change). Verified by re-running the full E-track suite: `node --test tests/packs/*.test.mjs tests/orchestration/persona-degraded.test.mjs tests/embed-authority-binding.test.mjs tests/embed-authority-guard.test.mjs` — 145 tests, 0 failures. No production code changed by this closeout.
- LMCP-C10: capability→test edges in the living graph (2026-07-03, `construct-9oi4.3.10`). `lib/graph/build-from-corpus.mjs` extracts `test --validates--> capability` edges from `@capability` annotations across the whole test corpus (via `lib/test-corpus-inventory.mjs`'s new `extractCapabilityTestEdges`), merging with the existing registry-sourced (`verification.functional`/`hostEmulation`) edges rather than replacing them — a capability tagged only in a ledger entry (`tests/capabilities/ledger.json`) gets its capability node created here, since nothing else in the graph does; an annotation matching neither the registry nor the ledger is reported as orphaned, not fabricated into a node. `lib/graph/gaps.mjs` adds `findMissingTestCapabilities()` — capabilities/workflows with zero inbound `validates` edges — wired to `construct graph query --missing-tests [--json]` in `lib/graph/cli.mjs`. Backfilled `@capability` annotations across 12 flagship functional suites (workflow-invoke, triage, orchestration-mcp, mcp-parity, mcp-ingest-resilience, host-config-parity, host-mcp-emulation, docling-remote-ingest, node-native-extraction, oracle-bounded-auto) plus `tests/capability-tier.test.mjs` / `tests/model-router-local.test.mjs` for `local.model.tier` — the one registry capability whose only verification is unit/integration, not functional/hostEmulation, and so previously had zero graph edges. `construct graph query --missing-tests --json` now returns zero capabilities and zero workflows against the live repo. Tests: `tests/graph/build-from-corpus.test.mjs`, `tests/graph/gaps.test.mjs`, `tests/graph/cli.test.mjs` (13 new tests; full `tests/graph/` suite: 63 passing).
- Queue reframed as a provider-kind contract (2026-07-03, `construct-9oi4.7.11`, supersedes the ADR-0051 git-vs-postgres framing). Substrate owns the `kind:'queue'` CONTRACT; git-queue is the zero-dependency DEFAULT provider (not deprecated) and Postgres is an OPTIONAL provider selected explicitly through the extension registry. `lib/deployment-mode.mjs` declares `queue: 'git'` for all modes; the silent `postgres`→`git` alias is removed from `lib/intake/queue.mjs`, where selection now resolves through the registry by kind (`lib/intake/queue-registry.mjs`) and an unknown backend throws `UnregisteredQueueProviderError` instead of downgrading silently. `lib/intake/git-queue.mjs` `claim()` reports a typed non-durable disposition (`durable:false, disposition:'local-only'`) on push failure rather than ratifying it as durable, and `durable:true, disposition:'pushed'` on success. `lib/orchestration/run-store-postgres.mjs` is tagged as an UNREGISTERED `kind:'queue'` provider scaffold. ADR-0051 carries a superseding amendment note. Tests updated: `tests/git-queue-disposition.test.mjs`, `tests/deployment-mode.test.mjs`.
- LMCP Wave 5 recovery landed (2026-07-03, closes `construct-9oi4.15.6` tracker-branch reconciliation): committed the working-tree evidence for already-closed beads H2 (`lib/identity.mjs` actor/service identity), I2 (durable `awaiting_approval` state in `lib/embed/approval-queue.mjs` + `construct approvals` CLI in `lib/cli/approvals.mjs`), N6 (`lib/mcp/destructive-gate.mjs` out-of-band approval-token gate for destructive MCP tools), K5/L3/L4 acceptance tests (`tests/acceptance/global-install.test.mjs`, `05-optional-dep-degradation.test.mjs`, `k5-ink-react-degradation.functional.test.mjs`), plus broker/policy wiring in `lib/mcp/{broker,server}.mjs` and `lib/policy/engine.mjs`. MCP CallTool ordering: rate-limit check now runs before the destructive gate so unauthorized destructive floods still consume their class budget.
- ADR-0051 runtime alignment: `lib/deployment-mode.mjs` now declares `queue: 'git'` for team/enterprise and `lib/intake/queue.mjs` no longer treats the git queue as a degraded fallback requiring `CONSTRUCT_DEGRADED_OK=postgres-queue` — the git queue is the designated team backend. Tests updated to the ADR-0051 contract (`tests/deployment-mode.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/mode-honesty.functional.test.mjs`, `tests/intake-queue-factory.test.mjs`).

### Changed

- LMCP-C7: `construct status` and `construct doctor` now read workflow truth from the living graph (`construct-9oi4.3.7`) — status gains a Workflows section (available/degraded/missing sourced from `graph validate` + runtime-evidence) and doctor runs a graph-validate check (`lib/doctor/graph-validate.mjs`), so neither can silently disagree with the graph. Tests: `tests/status.test.mjs` + `tests/doctor-graph-validate.test.mjs` (8).
- LMCP-H5: mandatory audit + cross-tenant isolation for enterprise mode (`construct-9oi4.8.5`) — broker/policy/writes fail closed when the audit sink is unavailable (`lib/policy/audit-gate.mjs`), audit records carry tenant+actor (H1/H2), and a cross-tenant isolation suite (`lib/tenant/isolation.mjs`) proves tenant A artifacts are never readable via tenant B contexts across queue/store/memory/audit; `lib/mode-capabilities.mjs` `mandatory-audit` now reports implemented. Tests: `tests/enterprise/audit-isolation.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-I6: the policy engine (`lib/policy/engine.mjs`) now validates role claims against the resolved actor/service identity (H2) in team/enterprise mode via new `lib/policy/role-authority.mjs` (`construct-9oi4.9.6`) — an env-claimed role not in the identity's registered grants is denied fail-closed before any manifest lookup; solo mode unchanged (CONSTRUCT_ROLE stays a documented convenience). Tests: `tests/policy/role-authority.test.mjs` (18).
- LMCP-C6: graph staleness now hashes the full seed set per named source (`construct-9oi4.3.6`) — registry, `.cx` overlays, specialists/org, pack dirs, provider manifests (incl. `.cx/providers.json`), workflow manifests — so `graph stale` names exactly which source drifted, and it fixes a latent bug where directory-valued seeds never moved the hash (the old flat-file hasher treated any directory path as permanently missing). Tests: `tests/graph/staleness.test.mjs`.
- LMCP-D2: `lib/embedded-contract/workflow-defs.mjs` is now provably generated (`construct-9oi4.4.2`) — its catalog derives exclusively from `lib/workflows/loader.mjs` manifests, with a new `scripts/check-workflow-defs-drift.mjs` (`npm run workflow-defs:check`, wired into `release:check` and `construct sync`) that fails if hand-editing reintroduces a static catalog entry.
- LMCP-F5: the embed daemon now actually invokes specialist workflows over provider snapshots (`construct-9oi4.6.8`) — the per-capability scheduled job composes the specialist's bound provider snapshot slice (E4 bindings + B11 filters), requests a plan via `workflow-invoke.mjs`, validates output against the role's contract (F2), and enqueues E4-authorized write proposals as durable writeIntents in the approval queue, replacing the P2 stub. The daemon never calls a provider write adapter directly; with no reasoning engine wired yet, every tick is an honest `skipped-with-reason(reasoning-executor-not-available)`, never a fabricated completion. Tests: `tests/functional/embed-specialist-invocation.functional.test.mjs` (6).
- LMCP-H1: enterprise tenancy (`CONSTRUCT_TENANT_ID`/`deployment.tenantId`) now has real behavior (`construct-9oi4.8.1`) — a tenant context is resolved once from config+env (`lib/tenant/context.mjs`) and propagated onto orchestration run/task records and the intake queue factory. Solo/team default to explicit tenant `local`; enterprise mode with no resolvable tenant fails closed at plan/startup with an actionable error (ADR-0057/A7). Tests: `tests/enterprise/tenant.test.mjs` (19).
- LMCP-B3: the embed daemon's provider registry (`lib/embed/providers/registry.mjs`) is now a thin, manifest-driven view over the unified extension registry (`construct-9oi4.2.3`) instead of a second hardcoded env-var table. Linear gained a proper `data-source` manifest; missing-credential providers surface in `construct status` as "configured but unavailable" with a reason instead of being silently omitted.

### Fixed

- construct-tbiv: PreToolUse Bash hooks no longer fail with `Cannot find module '.../.construct/run.mjs'` (`node:internal/modules/cjs/loader:1478`) when a hook runs from a drifted working directory. The generated hook command anchored the launcher on `${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}` — the `.` fallback is cwd-relative, so whenever `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` was unset (any host that does not export it) *and* the hook fired from a directory other than the project root (a `cd` in a prior command, scratch dir, slash-command context), Node's module loader threw MODULE_NOT_FOUND before any code in `run.mjs` could run — which is why the reverted in-shim fix (ceb93a07) could not help: the file never loaded. `scripts/sync-specialists.mjs` (`makeHooksPortable`) now bakes the absolute project root as the fallback (`${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-<absRoot>}/.construct/run.mjs`), keeping `$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` as the primary anchor (correct if the checkout moves) while making the fallback cwd-independent. `.claude/settings.json` is gitignored and regenerated per machine by `construct sync`, so the absolute fallback is machine-correct without leaking a shared path — the same shape the global `~/.claude/settings.json` already uses. Verified by reproducing the original failure (`cd /tmp`, `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` unset) against the new command: exit 0, launcher resolves. Tests: `tests/distribution-bootstrap.test.mjs`, `tests/sync-contract.test.mjs` now assert the fallback is an absolute path (not `:-.`).
- construct-r8wr.6: capability-freshness false positives from a coupled mtime + over-inclusive-closure defect in `lib/oracle/read-model.mjs` — the "capability(ies) have implementation changes since last validation" gate compared `fs.statSync(...).mtimeMs` against `lastValidated`, which any checkout/rebase/touch rewrites repo-wide with no content change, and it read realizers via the full advisory `realizes` closure that `lib/graph/build-import-graph.mjs` deliberately over-generates for impact tracing (hundreds of transitive files per capability). Both defects compounded: mtime noise applied across an over-broad file set guaranteed spurious `impact-untested` flags and the resulting `>=5 -> high` escalation. The freshness consumer now (1) reads only direct, registry-sourced `realizes` edges (`source: 'registry'`, the capability's declared impl files) instead of the transitive closure, and (2) replaces mtime with content identity — a realizer counts as changed only if it differs from git HEAD (dirty working tree) or a commit touching it landed after `lastValidated` (`git log --since`) — so a fresh checkout or bare `touch` flags nothing. The advisory closure and `build-import-graph.mjs` edges are unchanged; only the validation-grade consumer was narrowed. `collectDependencyGraph` is now exported from `lib/oracle/read-model.mjs` for direct testing. Tests: `tests/oracle-capability-freshness.test.mjs` (5) — touch-only no-op, simulated fresh-checkout no-op, real content change flags exactly its own capability, committed change after the stamp flags it, and a change to a file reachable only through the advisory closure does not leak into the gate.
- AP2.7: `mcp-health-check` now honors its own warn-only contract (`construct-1yhp.7`) — a single tool-level failure no longer marks the whole MCP server unhealthy and blocking. `lib/hooks/mcp-health-check.mjs`'s PreToolUse path always exits 0 (a stderr notice is emitted while failures are recent, never `exit 2`); the `@exits` header now states this accurately. The failures counter, which previously never reset, now clears back to `0`/`healthy` once a subsequent tool use finds the failure window expired. Tests: `tests/hooks/mcp-health-check-warn-only.test.mjs` (4) — warn-only on single and repeated failures, counter reset after the window elapses, non-MCP passthrough untouched.
- AP3.1: model registry is reachable on the embedded resolution path (`construct-uccl.1`) — the `registryPath` param threaded through `resolveExecution`/`resolveEmbeddedModel` was never supplied by any call site, so `specialists/org/models.json` was structurally dead config. `resolveEmbeddedModel` now defaults `registryPath` to `defaultModelRegistryPath(env)` (`lib/model-router.mjs`: `CX_TOOLKIT_DIR` wins, else the installed package root), so a dropped-in `specialists/org/models.json` binds tier defaults on every surface. Precedence is unchanged and now documented: a `CX_MODEL_<TIER>`/`CONSTRUCT_MODEL_<TIER>` env pin always wins; the registry fills only tiers no env pin sets. Construct still ships **no active registry** (a starter template lives at `specialists/org/models.json.example`), so an unconfigured machine degrades to a `config-error` (no implicit defaults, ADR-0027) rather than resolving a model whose credentials may be absent. Tests: `tests/functional/model-registry-wiring.functional.test.mjs` (3).
- construct-uccl.4: the validated-model subsystem in `lib/model-router.mjs` (`resolveValidatedModel`/`recommendTierModel`/`formatModelResolutionNotices`/`isModelAvailable`, `RETIRED_MODEL_SLUGS`) had zero external callers, so a retired slug pinned via `CX_MODEL_STANDARD` resolved silently. `lib/embedded-contract/model-resolve.mjs`'s tier-default branch now validates the resolved pin with `isModelAvailable(..., { strictPin: true })` and, on rejection, routes through `resolveValidatedModel` to surface a named warning (slug + suggested replacement) via `formatModelResolutionNotices`; a valid pin resolves unchanged with no warning. Test: `tests/model-router-validated-wiring.test.mjs` (3 passing).
- construct-uccl.2: `lib/embedded-contract/model-resolve.mjs` now consults `listModelFamilies` for a configured provider credential when no CX_MODEL_*/registry pin resolves, falling back to that family's tier default with a named warning and a distinct `resolutionSource: 'credential-family-fallback'` — an explicit pin always suppresses the fallback, and with neither a pin nor a credential the remediation names the exact env var/command to run. Fixed two ambient-credential probes in `lib/model-router.mjs`'s `isProviderConfigured` (the Ollama localhost curl check and the GitHub Copilot `gh auth status`/credential-store check) that ran unconditionally regardless of `allowAmbient`, which would otherwise leak into hermetic resolution paths. Test: `tests/functional/model-resolver-credential-fallback.functional.test.mjs` (4 passing); updated `tests/functional/embedded-contract-model-resolve.functional.test.mjs`'s config-error assertions to scrub PATH/ambient provider access so an authenticated dev machine (`gh auth status`) doesn't regress the no-credential control cases.
- construct-uccl.3: `lib/hooks/model-fallback.mjs` called `selectFallbackModel` without `registryModels`, so the fallback candidate list was always empty and a retryable provider failure could never select a next model; the hook now reads `specialists/org/models.json` under `CX_TOOLKIT_DIR` (mirroring `resolveModelTiers`'s registry path) before selecting a fallback. Removed three no-op cooldown writes on paths where no fallback was actually applied (no candidate resolved, construct binary missing, no OpenRouter key) — only the post-apply cooldown write survives, so a no-op hook run no longer suppresses a provider on the next failure. Test: `tests/hooks/model-fallback-registry.test.mjs` (3 passing).
- construct-uccl.5: `lib/config/project-config.mjs` reverts the entire `construct.config.json` to defaults on any validation error (per ADR-0021), but `lib/orchestration/runtime.mjs`'s `loadConfig` discarded the `errors`/`source` that reversion carries — a typo like `store: "sqllite"` silently flipped `workerBackend` back to `inline` with no user-visible signal. `planRun` now surfaces a warning naming the offending key/value and stating defaults were applied; `construct doctor`'s config check prints the first validation error plus a "+N more" count instead of a bare error count. Test: `tests/functional/config-invalid-surfaces-warning.functional.test.mjs` (3 passing).
- construct-1yhp.1: `lib/orchestration/readiness.mjs`'s verdict chain checked tool attachment/version/auth only, so a green `construct orchestrate preflight` could precede a run that degrades with "No model could be resolved". `buildOrchestrationReadiness` now invokes the same resolution `orchestration_run` uses (`resolveEmbeddedModel` per tier, `resolveExecution`, the newly-exported `resolveWorkerBackend`, `resolveWebCapability`) against the same env — no network calls — and adds bundle fields (`modelResolved`, `credentialMaterializable`, `workerBackend`, `webMode`) plus two reason codes (`model_unresolved`, `execution_degraded`) with actionable next steps. Surfaced in `construct orchestrate preflight`'s text and JSON output. Updated two pre-existing tests in `tests/orchestration-readiness.test.mjs` that assumed tool-attachment alone implied PASS. Test: `tests/functional/orchestration-readiness-executability.functional.test.mjs` (4 passing, including verdict/planRun parity).
- construct-1yhp.5: wired `probeProviderConnection` (`lib/providers/connection-probe.mjs`) into `construct doctor --probe-providers` and `construct orchestrate preflight --deep` as opt-in deep credential checks — default paths stay presence-first (zero `op read`, zero network) per construct-trxz's prompt-storm constraint. `creds list` and `capability_describe` (`lib/embedded-contract/capability.mjs`) now label provider presence as "configured (unverified ref)" / `configuredBasis: 'presence'` rather than a bare checkmark. Test: `tests/functional/doctor-probe-providers.functional.test.mjs` (5 passing, using a fetch-spy preload to prove zero outbound calls on default paths and a real probe call under the opt-in flags).
- LMCP-I3: MCP broker denials are durable evidence (`construct-9oi4.9.3`) — denied and approval-required policy decisions now persist as schema-complete records (`decisionId`/`actor`/`tenant`/`project`/`tool`/`target`/`risk`/`outcome`/`correlationId`/`ts`) in a new `lib/mcp/denied-store.mjs` and the audit trail, and a `correlationId` ties every broker decision to its `tool.called` trace event. Reuses H2 identity, H1 tenant, and N3 redaction. Tests: `tests/mcp/denial-audit.test.mjs` (12).
- LMCP-L5: `test:visual` no longer prints a static string and exits 0 doing nothing (`construct-9oi4.12.5`). It now runs a real rendered-artifact gate (`lib/render-visual-check.mjs`): exports PDF/DOCX/PPTX via the real Pandoc/Typst/pptxgenjs/LibreOffice engines, asserts every rasterized page carries real ink (zlib+PNG-filter pixel decode, no image dependency), and reuses `runOutputQuality`'s text-parity and reference-integrity checks. A required engine that is absent is a hard, CI-visible failure, never a silent skip. Tests: `tests/visual/rendered-artifact.test.mjs` (6).
- `construct graph` no longer shadows the intake task-graph command: the two disjoint subcommand families (dependency graph `build|stat|query|validate` vs task graph `list|show|from-intake|status|recommend`) dispatch from a single handler in `bin/construct`; the duplicate internal `graph` catalog entry was merged into the public one (`lib/cli-commands.mjs`).
- ADR-0035 marked `superseded` (by ADR-0058) so the live decision tree's supersede chains validate; ADRs 0051–0059 added to the ADR README index and `docs/decisions/adr/meta.json` nav (clears `03-docs:orphaned-doc` findings).
- `registry/capabilities.json` gains a `workflow.triage` entry so all 11 embedded workflow types have registry coverage; catalog edges regenerated.
- `tests/storage-health-probe.test.mjs` updated for the async LanceDB open probe (M1) with injected opener stubs, including a new unhealthy-open case; functional-layer count check in `tests/audit/f12-gates/audit-doc-staleness.test.mjs` now mirrors the inventory's definition (`tests/functional/` or `*.functional.test.mjs`).
- Audit ratchet baseline: accepted `module-test-only` for `lib/security/{ingest-boundary,recall-wrapper}.mjs` and `lib/writes/envelope.mjs` — runtime wiring is tracked by open beads (J3–J6, N-track); removal tightens the ratchet.

- LMCP Wave 2 implementation (2026-07-03): 6 beads closed (`construct-9oi4.8.6`, `construct-9oi4.13.1`, `construct-9oi4.13.3`, `construct-9oi4.10.1`, `construct-9oi4.7.8`, `construct-9oi4.9.1`). H6: enterprise status/doctor fail-closed — per-capability table + unsupported verdict in enterprise mode. M1: LanceDB open probe replaces stat-only storage health check (async, 2s timeout, TTL cache). M3: docs contradiction sweep — brokered MCP marked staged/experimental, provider capability matrix corrected. J1: fake-provider test harness for GitHub/Jira/Confluence with failure modes (43 tests). G8: no silent team→solo fallback — `requireTeamCapabilityOrDegrade()` helper, explicit degradation records. I1: persistent broker singleton with file-backed rate-limit state (BrokerStore).

- LMCP Wave 3 implementation (2026-07-03): 6 beads closed (`construct-9oi4.12.2`, `construct-9oi4.8.3`, `construct-9oi4.13.2`, `construct-9oi4.13.4`, `construct-9oi4.11.1`, `construct-9oi4.7.7`). L2: packed consumer install test (npm pack → clean install → run construct version/status/doctor, 9 tests). H3: deny-by-default policy for team/enterprise (solo permissive, team deny-unclassified, enterprise deny). M2: `degradationDetails` in status from `.cx/degradation.jsonl`. M4: doctor stub/not-implemented tier (`classifyCapability`, `~` icon). K1: dependency-intent manifest (34 entries in `deps/intent.json`, CI validator). G7: git-queue disposition per A1 — correctness envelope, push-failure logging, markSkipped(), read() fix.

- LMCP Wave 4 implementation (2026-07-03): 3 beads closed (`construct-9oi4.14.1`, `construct-9oi4.12.1`, `construct-9oi4.2.1`). N1: trust/provenance labeling at ingest boundaries — trust taxonomy (TRUST_LEVELS), `stampTrust`, `stampIngestBoundary`, `wrapForContextAssembly` recall grading; promotes F08 red fixture (26 tests). L1: acceptance contract suite scaffold — 4 workflow contract specs (init, provider health, status, doctor) + existing packed-install test (56 tests total). B1: extension manifest schema (`lib/extensions/`) — `validateManifest`, `loadManifestsFromDir`, `mergeManifests`, doctor check, built-in echo.manifest.json example (14 tests).

- LMCP Track A decisions (ADR-0051..0059, 2026-07-03): all nine architecture decision beads (`construct-9oi4.1.1`..`.1.9`) closed with ADRs. A1: git-queue as the formal team backend (Postgres deprecated as a future provider-plugin path). A2: unified provider/extension manifest schema and kind taxonomy covering all 9+ registries. A3: living graph extended node/edge taxonomy, JSONL store, CI drift gate, and doctor integration. A4: workflow manifest schema and precedence rules; `workflow-defs.mjs` becomes a generated shim. A5: pack manifest schema, prompt resolution order, and hard-failure policy for missing prompts in team/enterprise. A6: deny-by-default policy, durable approval/audit state schema, and actor identity propagation. A7: four-way enterprise capability partition (implement-now/fail-closed/later/not-advertised). A8: acceptance-first test rebuild strategy with triage rubric, deletion waves, and red-fixture promotion contract. A9: dependency-intent rubric, disposition options, and quarantine mechanism for undeclared sidecars.

- Living Modular Control Plane audit backlog (branch `audit/living-modular-control-plane-beads`, 2026-07-03): full three-agent source audit of staging validated the mode/queue/broker/policy/registry/test/docs claims, and a 114-bead dependency-linked backlog was created in the beads tracker under umbrella epic `construct-9oi4` — 15 track epics (A architecture decisions, B unified provider/extension system, C living workflow/capability graph, D workflow manifest normalization, E pack boundary, F specialist execution provenance, G team mode, H staged enterprise, I MCP/policy/approval hardening, J governed external writes, K dependency intent, L acceptance-first test rebuild, M status/doctor/docs truth, N security hardening, O migration/removal) with 98 child beads, 9 Opus-only decision beads gating downstream tracks, and 119 dependency links (`bd dep cycles` clean). Tracker hygiene executed alongside (LMCP-O1, closed): five duplicate same-day epic pairs superseded (`construct-imsa`→`f8w6`, `etgf`→`uccl`, `izp7`→`fbxv`, `slbd`→`neq9`, `tzo1`→`r8wr`) and three mutual `relates_to` links removed (`bcbo.4`⇄`bcbo.8`, `bcbo.4`⇄`bcbo.10`, `bcbo.6`⇄`bcbo.7`, preserved as notes) that made `bd list` tree rendering recurse infinitely.

### Fixed

- Repaired collision-reverts introduced while collecting the parallel fvw5 remediation waves, where agent worktrees that branched before earlier AP-remediation work silently reverted it on collection. Restored from the last-good commit `9c2babbc`: `lib/mcp/tools/orchestration-run.mjs` (re-exports `shapeRun`/`DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`, restores the bounded `statusViaService`/`runViaService` remote-request timeout and `RemoteFetchTimeoutError`) and `lib/orchestration/runtime.mjs` (restores the honest terminal-state derivation — `degraded`/`completed-prepare-only` instead of bare `completed`), re-applying the third-wave `planRun` degraded-store stderr line on top. Restored `bin/construct` from HEAD and re-applied only X2/D4 plus the 1yhp.4 self-probe scope fix (`callerSuppliedObservations` → `local-probe`), preserving the A2/A3 `PROJECT_ROOT` wiring and the B2 mode-capability doctor check that the stale worktree had dropped. In `lib/mcp/server.mjs` restored the `local-probe` enum value, the `{ cwd: ROOT_DIR }` argument on the `mcpBroker`/`mcpBrokerMode`/dispatch `isBrokered` calls, and the `profile-lifecycle.md` reference. In `docs/guides/concepts/architecture.mdx` restored HEAD's XDG paths and honest Point-at text, re-applying only the root-semantics paragraph and service-manager correction. Because the same regressing commit had also rewritten `tests/orchestration-runtime.test.mjs` to expect the regressed `completed` where the honest taxonomy returns `completed-prepare-only`, reverted those three assertions to `completed-prepare-only` (consistent with the AP terminal-state audit tests). Preserved the legitimate E2 work that the same commit introduced: re-added `runMode = 'in-process'` to `startRun` (with an explicit `store.saveRun`) in `lib/orchestration/runtime.mjs` and `runMode: run.runMode ?? null` to `shapeRun` in `lib/mcp/tools/orchestration-run.mjs`, so `orchestration_status` still labels solo in-process runs. Restores green on `tests/audit/f09-orchestration/{degraded-flag-propagation,remote-status-timeout,status-path-truthfulness}.test.mjs`, `tests/functional/{honest-terminal-states,preflight-scope-label}.functional.test.mjs`, `tests/orchestration-runtime.test.mjs`.
- Removed both hardcoded storage-health false-greens (closes `construct-puq8`). `probeStorageHealth(cwd, { fsExistsSync })` in `lib/status.mjs` (wired into `buildStatus`) and `getStorageStatus()` in `lib/storage/admin.mjs` now derive status from filesystem state instead of returning a constant `healthy`: `.cx/` absent → `unavailable`, `.cx/` present without `.cx/lancedb` → `degraded`, both present → `healthy` (honoring `CONSTRUCT_LANCEDB_PATH`). Proven by `tests/storage-health-probe.test.mjs` (4 tests) and `tests/storage-admin-status-probe.test.mjs` (4 tests).
- Regenerated `tests/capabilities/corpus-inventory.json` (and `tests/AUDIT.md`) to index the fvw5 test files (`node scripts/generate-test-corpus-inventory.mjs`); prior waves committed the tests without regenerating the inventory. Registered `docs/guides/concepts/root-semantics.md` in `docs/guides/concepts/meta.json` so it is reachable from the docs nav (clears the `03-docs:orphaned-doc` audit finding).

- Documentation truth pass (fvw5.25/X1): corrected 5 wrong claims across 5 files — `README.md` uninstall section referenced `~/.construct/` and Postgres container (now `~/.local/state/construct/`, no Postgres); `docs/guides/start/index.mdx` quickstart implied Docker/Postgres prerequisite (now describes embedded LanceDB); `docs/guides/concepts/architecture.mdx` service-manager description and machine-scope path table corrected to XDG paths and LanceDB; `docs/decisions/adr/0014-local-embeddings-optional.md` cited a wrong exception list for ADR-0001 (corrected with Status update paragraph).
- Single-source mode health invariant proven (fvw5.17/X3): `lib/mode-capabilities.mjs` now exports `CAPABILITY_STATUSES`, `_injectCapabilityForTesting`, and `CAPABILITY_REGISTRY`. `tests/mode-health-single-source.test.mjs` (12 tests) proves that injecting a capability into the registry surfaces in both `getModeCapabilityStatus()` and `buildStatus()` without touching any other file.
- Optional dep graceful degradation confirmed (fvw5.23/F2): CLI critical path (`bin/construct`, `lib/cli-commands.mjs`, `lib/mcp/server.mjs`) has zero static ink/react imports. `tests/consumer/optional-deps-absent.test.mjs` (7 tests) acts as a ratchet guard preventing accidental static imports, and verifies `construct version` and `construct evals --json` exit 0.
- Added doctor check for half-initialized Beads state (fvw5.21/D4): `bin/construct` cmdDoctor now detects when `.claude/settings.json` references `bd` but `.beads/` is absent or has no database file, failing the check with a repair instruction (`bd init`). Proven by `tests/doctor/beads-half-staged.test.mjs` (6 tests).
- Functional broker enforcement tests (fvw5.20/C2): `tests/functional/mcp-broker-enforcement.functional.test.mjs` (7 tests) verifies policy deny blocks `execute`, policy approve calls it and emits a trace event, solo bypasses the broker entirely, and `isBrokered()` returns the correct value for each deployment mode.
- Fixed `mcpBroker` status field to use `isBrokered(process.env)` instead of a raw env check, and added `mcpBrokerMode: 'dispatch' | 'off'` to the MCP resource output so the actual dispatch path is observable (fvw5.16/C3). Proven by `tests/mcp-broker-status.test.mjs` (6 tests).
- Routed MCP tool calls through `Broker.invoke()` in team/enterprise mode (fvw5.3/C1). The `CallToolRequestSchema` handler in `lib/mcp/server.mjs` previously called `dispatchToolByName` directly even when `isBrokered()` returned true, bypassing policy checks, rate limits, and audit-log events. Solo mode retains direct dispatch; team/enterprise routes through a `Broker` instance that enforces policy first — a denied decision throws before the tool implementation ever runs. Proven by `tests/mcp-broker-dispatch.test.mjs` (5 tests).
- Eliminated two false-green hardcodes in `bin/construct` cmdDoctor (fvw5.9/X2): `add('npm available', true)` now calls `spawnSync` to probe whether npm is on PATH; `add('performance-reviews/ ready', true)` now uses `fs.existsSync` as the passing predicate rather than asserting success after a `mkdirSync` side-effect. Remaining `sqlHealth` false-green in `lib/status.mjs` tracked as construct-puq8.
- **B4 — Mode-degraded fallback guard**: team/enterprise deployment modes can no longer silently fall back to solo behavior. `resolveRunStore` (lib/orchestration/store.mjs) returns `{ degraded: true, degradedReason: 'postgres-unavailable', warnings }` when postgres is requested but unavailable. `buildStatus()` wires `capabilityStatus` and `unsupportedCapabilities` from the mode-capabilities registry into the deployment status block. Proven by `tests/functional/mode-degraded-fallback.functional.test.mjs` (7 tests).
